103: /// Face cap of the pure-bend fit patch.
104: const BEND_PATCH_CAP: usize = 64;
105: 
106: /// How far a surface of revolution's support must sweep around its own axis, in
107: /// degrees, before the radius counts as determined by the data.
108: ///
109: /// This is an *identifiability* test, and worth naming as such because the
110: /// distinction is easy to lose: it does not ask which surface fits best, it
111: /// asks whether the data pins the parameter at all. A fit whose parameter is
112: /// unpinned is not a slightly-worse explanation, it is a free variable that
113: /// happened to land somewhere, and no amount of comparing residuals or coverage
114: /// between candidates can detect it — see the note on complexity pricing in
115: /// TODO.md for why the obvious model-selection fix does not work here.
116: ///
117: /// A patch subtending a negligible angle about the axis never observes the
118: /// revolution, so the radius is free — and at the limit of a free radius each
119: /// type collapses into the simpler one below it: a torus with R → ∞ is a
120: /// cylinder, a cylinder with r → ∞ is a plane. Such a fit is not merely
121: /// useless, it actively wins. Being a super-model of the simpler surface it
122: /// covers everything that surface covers, then floods a little further on the
123: /// slack — over the first tessellation row past a tangent junction, whose
124: /// chordal sag the simpler surface cannot take — and so carries the
125: /// largest-validated-area contest every time.
126: ///
127: /// Measured on `stepped_block_toroidal_fillets` fine, where neither population
128: /// overlaps by anything close to this bar:
129: ///
130: /// - **Tori.** The 18 real corner blends sweep 84.7°–103.4° and fit R to the
131: ///   reference's exact 2.0000 / 2.5000 / 14.0000. Six impostors astride the
132: ///   r = 0.5 and r = 1 perimeter fillets sweep 3.1°–6.7° and report
133: ///   R = 20, 26, 29, 37, 87, 114 — junk, each an unconstrained parameter's
134: ///   arbitrary landing place, and every one of them inside the model's own
135: ///   121 mm bounding box, which is why bounding the radius never caught them.
136: /// - **Cylinders.** Every real one sweeps ≥ 64.8°. Four impostors sweep
137: ///   2.8°–2.9°, each a flat wall plus the first tessellation row of the four
138: ///   fillets bordering it (those rows sit 2.9e-3 mm off the wall plane, over
139: ///   the flood tolerance, so the plane that should have won them cannot).
140: ///
141: /// 20° sits 3× above every impostor and 3× below every real surface. Note that
142: /// sagitta-versus-tolerance does *not* separate them — the toroidal impostors'
143: /// spines still deviate 0.016–0.042 mm, well above the flood tolerance. The
144: /// angle is what matters, not the sag.
145: ///
146: /// This is the counterpart to `MIN_HALF_ANGLE_DEG` in conical.rs, which rejects
147: /// a cone too shallow to be told apart from a cylinder.
148: const MIN_AXIAL_SWEEP_DEG: f64 = 20.0;
149: 
150: /// How many flood-start faces a toroidal candidate tries before giving up
151: /// (each attempt refits from the same initial patch, so extras only matter
152: /// when the best start happens not to validate its own fit).
153: const MAX_TORUS_STARTS: usize = 4;
154: 
155: /// A committed analytic hypothesis from competition, tagged by type.
156: pub(super) enum CompeteHyp {
157:     Planar(PlanarHypothesis),
158:     Conical(ConicalHypothesis),
159:     Spherical(SphericalHypothesis),
160:     Toroidal(ToroidalHypothesis),
161: }
162: 
163: impl CompeteHyp {
164:     pub(super) fn faces(&self) -> &[usize] {
165:         match self {
166:             CompeteHyp::Planar(h) => &h.faces,
167:             CompeteHyp::Conical(h) => &h.faces,
168:             CompeteHyp::Spherical(h) => &h.faces,
169:             CompeteHyp::Toroidal(h) => &h.faces,
170:         }
171:     }
172:     /// Short surface-type name for diagnostics.
173:     fn kind_name(&self) -> &'static str {
174:         match self {
175:             CompeteHyp::Planar(_) => "plane",
176:             CompeteHyp::Conical(h) => {
177:                 if h.is_cylinder() {
178:                     "cyl"
179:                 } else {
180:                     "cone"
181:                 }
182:             }
183:             CompeteHyp::Spherical(_) => "sphere",
184:             CompeteHyp::Toroidal(_) => "torus",
185:         }
186:     }
187:     /// Simplest-first rank for claim resolution (a face both a cylinder and a
188:     /// torus explain within tolerance is the cylinder's). Matches the ladder's
189:     /// ascending-DOF order.
190:     fn dof_rank(&self) -> u8 {
191:         match self {
192:             CompeteHyp::Planar(_) => 0,
193:             CompeteHyp::Spherical(_) => 1,
194:             CompeteHyp::Conical(_) => 2,
195:             CompeteHyp::Toroidal(_) => 3,
196:         }
197:     }
198:     fn set_faces(&mut self, faces: Vec<usize>, verts: Vec<usize>) {
199:         match self {
200:             CompeteHyp::Planar(h) => {
201:                 h.faces = faces;
202:                 h.vertices = verts;
203:             }
204:             CompeteHyp::Conical(h) => {
205:                 h.faces = faces;
206:                 h.vertices = verts;
207:             }
208:             CompeteHyp::Spherical(h) => {
209:                 h.faces = faces;
210:                 h.vertices = verts;
211:             }
212:             CompeteHyp::Toroidal(h) => {
213:                 h.faces = faces;
214:                 h.vertices = verts;
215:             }
216:         }
217:     }
218: }
219: 
220: /// Decompose a tangent-chain region. Returns the committed analytic
221: /// hypotheses (a strict partition after claim resolution) plus the leftover
222: /// faces no hypothesis explained.
223: pub(super) fn compete_region(
224:     mesh: &ConnectedMesh,
225:     region: &Region,
226:     jet: &[JetField],
227:     centroids: &[[f64; 3]],
228:     config: &Config,
229: ) -> (Vec<CompeteHyp>, Vec<usize>) {
230:     let n = mesh.faces.len();
231:     let debug = config.verbosity >= 3 || std::env::var("BREPPER_DEBUG_COMPETE").is_ok();
232:     let mut in_region = vec![false; n];
233:     for &fi in &region.faces {
234:         in_region[fi] = true;
235:     }
236: 
237:     // Region curvature scale: a face reads "flat" when its principal
238:     // curvature radius exceeds a couple of region diameters.
239:     let extent = region_extent(mesh, region);
240:     let flat_eps = if extent > 0.0 { 1.0 / (2.0 * extent) } else { 1e-6 };
241: 
242:     // Seed order: jet-interior-first (ascending curvature mismatch with
243:     // region neighbors), ties by face index for determinism.
244:     let mut seeds = region.faces.clone();
245:     let score: Vec<f64> = (0..n)
246:         .map(|fi| {
247:             if !in_region[fi] {
248:                 return f64::INFINITY;
249:             }
250:             neighbor_jet_mismatch(mesh, fi, jet, &in_region)
251:         })
252:         .collect();
253:     seeds.sort_by(|&a, &b| {
254:         score[a]
255:             .partial_cmp(&score[b])
256:             .unwrap()
257:             .then_with(|| a.cmp(&b))
258:     });
259: 
260:     // Every region face seeds exactly once — `seeds` is a permutation of them,
261:     // which is what terminates the loop. Committing a hypothesis does NOT
262:     // remove the faces it grew into from the seed pool.
263:     //
264:     // Growing into a face is a hypothesis, not a conclusion. The committed
265:     // face set is always a `flood` output, so every member validates — but
266:     // against a fit whose parameters came from an unvalidated seed
267:     // neighbourhood, so "validated" does not mean "the right surface". Letting
268:     // those faces seed too means a wrong hypothesis can be out-competed by a
269:     // right one grown from a face it had swallowed; `resolve_claims` already
270:     // exists to arbitrate the multi-claims that produces.
271:     //
272:     // It also makes the long-standing claim that seed misordering "costs only
273:     // time" actually true: previously an early bad hypothesis consumed faces a
274:     // later, better seed needed.
275:     let mut committed: Vec<CompeteHyp> = Vec::new();
276:     let mut claim_lists: Vec<Vec<usize>> = vec![Vec::new(); n]; // face -> committed idxs
277:     let mut claimed = vec![false; n];
278: 
279:     for &seed in &seeds {
280:         // Skip only faces in the INTERIOR of what is already explained: every
281:         // neighbour claimed too. Seeding there cannot produce a competitor —
282:         // a fit grown from the middle of a validated surface refits that same
283:         // surface — so it is pure cost. Faces at the margin still seed.
284:         if claimed[seed] && interior_of_claims(mesh, seed, &claimed) {
285:             continue;
286:         }
287:         let jf = jet[seed];
288:         // Dominant / secondary principal-curvature MAGNITUDES. The jet orders
289:         // k1 ≥ k2 by signed value, so for a concave feature (a hole wall,
290:         // κ = −1/r) the dominant curvature is negative and lands in k2 —
291:         // taking |k1| as the dominant would read the wall's flat axial
292:         // direction and treat the hole as planar. Order by magnitude instead.
293:         let a1 = jf.k1.abs().max(jf.k2.abs());
294:         let a2 = jf.k1.abs().min(jf.k2.abs());
295: 
296:         // Grow the jet-pruned candidate types; keep the largest validated
297:         // area (simplest-DOF tiebreak).
298:         let mut best: Option<(CompeteHyp, f64, u8)> = None; // (hyp, area, dof)
299:         let consider = |hyp_area: Option<(CompeteHyp, f64)>, dof: u8, best: &mut Option<(CompeteHyp, f64, u8)>| {
300:             if let Some((h, area)) = hyp_area {
301:                 let sweep = candidate_sweep(mesh, &h);
302:                 if sweep < MIN_AXIAL_SWEEP_DEG.to_radians() {
303:                     if debug {
304:                         eprintln!(
305:                             "    compete: seed {seed} rejected {} sweeping only {:.1}\u{00b0} about its axis — radius undetermined ({} faces, area {area:.2})",
306:                             h.kind_name(),
307:                             sweep.to_degrees(),
308:                             h.faces().len(),
309:                         );
310:                     }
311:                     return;
312:                 }
313:                 let better = match best {
314:                     None => true,
315:                     Some((_, ba, bd)) => area > *ba + 1e-9 || ((area - *ba).abs() <= 1e-9 && dof < *bd),
316:                 };
317:                 if better {
318:                     *best = Some((h, area, dof));
319:                 }
320:             }
321:         };
322: 
323:         // Plane is always a candidate (cheap; catches misread flats).
324:         let plane = grow::<PlanarSurface>(mesh, &in_region, seed, jet, centroids, config, 1);
325:         let mut cone_area = None;
326:         let mut sphere_area = None;
327:         let mut torus_area = None;
328:         if a1 >= flat_eps {
329:             // Curved: a developable (cylinder/cone) is always plausible.
330:             let cone = grow::<ConicalSurface>(mesh, &in_region, seed, jet, centroids, config, MIN_CYLINDER_FACES);
331:             cone_area = cone.as_ref().map(|(_, a, f)| (*a, f.len()));
332: 
333:             // Toroidal candidate: a direct grow from this seed (fillet tori,
334:             // bend tubes), skipped when the seed reads sphere-like — every
335:             // adjacent quad-row band of a sphere lies near some torus, so a
336:             // (κ,κ) seed's torus is at best a bogus band that loses to the
337:             // sphere anyway. A torus seed reads |κ2| < |κ1| (κ2 varies
338:             // azimuthally around the tube; it only approaches κ1 near the
339:             // degenerate R≈r hole of a donut — a whole-region ladder case,
340:             // not a competition case).
341:             let mut torus = if a2 < 0.8 * a1 {
342:                 let patch = seed_patch(mesh, &in_region, seed, jet);
343:                 torus_candidate(mesh, &in_region, &patch, &[seed], centroids, config)
344:             } else {
345:                 None
346:             };
347:             // Bent-tube promotion: a cylinder that stalled against
348:             // tube-continuation faces is the developable prefix of a bent
349:             // tube; grow a torus from the pure-bend patch past the stall.
350:             if let Some((cyl, _, cyl_faces)) = cone.as_ref() {
351:                 if cyl.is_cylinder() {
352:                     let promoted =
353:                         promote_bend(mesh, &in_region, cyl, cyl_faces, jet, centroids, config);
354:                     if let Some(p) = promoted {
355:                         if torus.as_ref().is_none_or(|(_, a, _)| p.1 > *a) {
356:                             torus = Some(p);
357:                         }
358:                     }
359:                 }
360:             }
361:             torus_area = torus.as_ref().map(|(_, a, f)| (*a, f.len()));
362: 
363:             consider(cone.map(|(h, a, _)| (CompeteHyp::Conical(h), a)), 2, &mut best);
364:             // Curved in both directions → also try a sphere.
365:             if a2 >= 0.3 * a1 {
366:                 let sphere = grow::<SphericalSurface>(mesh, &in_region, seed, jet, centroids, config, MIN_SPHERE_FACES);
367:                 sphere_area = sphere.as_ref().map(|(_, a, f)| (*a, f.len()));
368:                 consider(sphere.map(|(h, a, _)| (CompeteHyp::Spherical(h), a)), 1, &mut best);
369:             }
370:             consider(torus.map(|(h, a, _)| (CompeteHyp::Toroidal(h), a)), 3, &mut best);
371:         }
372:         let plane_area = plane.as_ref().map(|(_, a, f)| (*a, f.len()));
373:         consider(plane.map(|(h, a, _)| (CompeteHyp::Planar(h), a)), 0, &mut best);
374: 
375:         if debug && best.is_none() {
376:             eprintln!(
377:                 "    compete: seed {seed} jet=({:.3},{:.3}) class {:?} → NO GROWTH (plane {plane_area:?} cone {cone_area:?} sphere {sphere_area:?} torus {torus_area:?})",
378:                 jf.k1, jf.k2, curvature_class(jf),
379:             );
380:         }
381: 
382:         if let Some((hyp, area, dof)) = best {
383:             if debug {
384:                 eprintln!(
385:                     "    compete: seed {seed} → {} ({} faces, area {area:.2}, dof {dof}, sweep {:.1}\u{00b0})",
386:                     hyp.kind_name(),
387:                     hyp.faces().len(),
388:                     match &hyp {
389:                         CompeteHyp::Toroidal(t) => torus_sweep(mesh, t).to_degrees(),
390:                         CompeteHyp::Conical(c) => conical_sweep(mesh, c).to_degrees(),
391:                         _ => 0.0,
392:                     }
393:                 );
394:             }
395:             let idx = committed.len();
396:             for &f in hyp.faces() {
397:                 claim_lists[f].push(idx);
398:                 claimed[f] = true;
399:             }
400:             committed.push(hyp);
401:         }
402:         // A seed that grows nothing analytic costs only its own turn.
403:     }
404: 
405:     resolve_claims(
406:         mesh,
407:         &region.faces,
408:         &mut committed,
409:         &claim_lists,
410:         config.vertex_tolerance_mm,
411:         config,
412:     )
413: }
414: 
415: /// Resolve multi-claimed faces to their best-fitting claimant (2× margin to
416: /// leave the larger claimant), rebuild each hypothesis's face/vertex lists,
417: /// drop hypotheses that lost all faces, and collect unclaimed leftovers.
418: /// Is every neighbour of `fi` claimed too? Such a face is interior to the
419: /// explained set and cannot seed anything new.
420: fn interior_of_claims(mesh: &ConnectedMesh, fi: usize, claimed: &[bool]) -> bool {
421:     let face = &mesh.faces[fi];
422:     let vc = face.vertex_count as usize;
423:     face.neighbors[..vc]
424:         .iter()
425:         .all(|&ni| ni < 0 || claimed[ni as usize])
426: }
427: 
428: /// Grow a cylinder whose axis DIRECTION is supplied by an already-committed
429: /// neighbour instead of being estimated from an unvalidated seed neighbourhood.
430: ///
431: /// This is the point of stage 2.3(d2). A free 5-DOF cylinder fit on a leftover
432: /// arc fails on the **axis**, not the radius: measured on the three fillet
433: /// cylinders `stepped_block_toroidal` fine loses, fixing only the axis
434: /// direction recovers r = 0.99998 / 0.99999 / 0.50008 against true 1.0 / 1.0 /
435: /// 0.5, with residuals five to ten times *under* vertex tolerance. With the
436: /// axis fixed the fit is a 3-DOF circle in the perpendicular plane, which is
437: /// well conditioned on a short arc — so growth can start from the seed facet
438: /// alone and there is no blind patch to straddle a junction.
439: pub(super) fn grow_with_axis(
440:     mesh: &ConnectedMesh,
441:     in_region: &[bool],
442:     seed: usize,
443:     axis: [f64; 3],
444:     centroids: &[[f64; 3]],
445:     config: &Config,
446: ) -> Option<(ConicalHypothesis, f64, Vec<usize>)> {
447:     let vtol = config.vertex_tolerance_mm;
448:     let stol = config.surface_tolerance_mm;
449:     let mut accepted = vec![seed];
450:     let mut fit = None;
451:     for _ in 0..MAX_GROW_ROUNDS {
452:         let verts = vertices_of(mesh, &accepted);
453:         let (center, radius) =
454:             super::cylindrical::fit_circle_for_axis(&axis, &verts, &mesh.vertices)?;
455:         let mut f = ConeFit {
456:             apex: center,
457:             axis_direction: axis,
458:             half_angle: 0.0,
459:             radius,
460:             convex: None,
461:         };
462:         ConicalSurface::determine_convexity(mesh, &accepted, &mut f);
463:         let grown = flood::<ConicalSurface>(mesh, in_region, seed, &f, centroids, vtol, stol);
464:         if grown.is_empty() {
465:             return None; // the seed does not lie on its own constrained fit
466:         }
467:         let done = grown.len() == accepted.len();
468:         accepted = grown;
469:         fit = Some(f);
470:         if done {
471:             break;
472:         }
473:     }
474:     let fit = fit?;
475:     if accepted.len() < MIN_CYLINDER_FACES {
476:         return None;
477:     }
478:     let area: f64 = accepted
479:         .iter()
480:         .map(|&fi| face_area(&mesh.faces[fi], &mesh.vertices))
481:         .sum();
482:     let verts = vertices_of(mesh, &accepted);
483:     let hyp = ConicalSurface::make_hypothesis(mesh, accepted.clone(), verts, &fit);
484:     Some((hyp, area, accepted))
485: }
486: 
487: fn resolve_claims(
488:     mesh: &ConnectedMesh,
489:     region_faces: &[usize],
490:     committed: &mut Vec<CompeteHyp>,
491:     claim_lists: &[Vec<usize>],
492:     vtol: f64,
493:     config: &Config,
494: ) -> (Vec<CompeteHyp>, Vec<usize>) {
495:     let hyp_area: Vec<f64> = committed
496:         .iter()
497:         .map(|h| h.faces().iter().map(|&fi| face_area(&mesh.faces[fi], &mesh.vertices)).sum())
498:         .collect();
499: 
500:     let mut assigned: Vec<Vec<usize>> = vec![Vec::new(); committed.len()];
501:     let mut leftovers: Vec<usize> = Vec::new();
502: 
503:     for &fi in region_faces {
504:         let claimants = &claim_lists[fi];
505:         if claimants.is_empty() {
506:             leftovers.push(fi);
507:             continue;
508:         }
509:         if claimants.len() == 1 {
510:             assigned[claimants[0]].push(fi);
511:             continue;
512:         }
513:         let dist = |ci: usize| max_vertex_dist(mesh, &committed[ci], fi);
514:         // Among claimants that ALREADY explain the face within vertex
515:         // tolerance, prefer the simplest surface (lowest DOF), then largest
516:         // area, then lowest index: a face on both a cylinder and a torus near
517:         // their tangency is the cylinder's; a face on a real fillet and a
518:         // spurious thin junction torus (both sub-micron) is the larger
519:         // fillet's. Comparing absolute best-fit here instead would let a tiny
520:         // tighter sliver steal it and fragment one surface into a chain.
521:         let within: Vec<usize> = claimants.iter().copied().filter(|&ci| dist(ci) <= vtol).collect();
522:         let winner = if !within.is_empty() {
523:             *within
524:                 .iter()
525:                 .min_by(|&&a, &&b| {
526:                     committed[a]
527:                         .dof_rank()
528:                         .cmp(&committed[b].dof_rank())
529:                         .then_with(|| hyp_area[b].partial_cmp(&hyp_area[a]).unwrap())
530:                         .then_with(|| a.cmp(&b))
531:                 })
532:                 .unwrap()
533:         } else {
534:             // No claimant fits within tolerance — best-fit, with a 2× margin
535:             // to leave the larger-area claimant (a true junction face fits
536:             // both sides equally; the margin prevents flapping).
537:             let mut order: Vec<usize> = claimants.clone();
538:             order.sort_by(|&a, &b| {
539:                 hyp_area[b].partial_cmp(&hyp_area[a]).unwrap().then_with(|| a.cmp(&b))
540:             });
541:             let mut winner = order[0];
542:             let mut winner_d = dist(winner);
543:             for &ci in &order[1..] {
544:                 let d = dist(ci);
545:                 if d * 2.0 < winner_d {
546:                     winner = ci;
547:                     winner_d = d;
548:                 }
549:             }
550:             winner
551:         };
552:         assigned[winner].push(fi);
553:     }
554: 
555:     let mut out = Vec::new();
556:     for (ci, mut hyp) in committed.drain(..).enumerate() {
557:         let faces = std::mem::take(&mut assigned[ci]);
558:         if faces.is_empty() {
559:             continue;
560:         }
561:         let verts = vertices_of(mesh, &faces);
562:         hyp.set_faces(faces, verts);
563:         // The fit was computed on the support the flood grew, which included
564:         // every face this hypothesis could reach — junction rows it has now
565:         // lost to a better claimant among them. Those rows are exactly the ones
566:         // that pull a fit off true, so a hypothesis that has just been trimmed
567:         // is carrying the error of faces it no longer owns. Refit on what it
568:         // actually kept. (Measured on rounded_cube fine, whose r = 2 edge
569:         // fillets read 1.9976–1.9984 with no vertex closer than 5e-4 mm to
570:         // their own surface, against a mesh whose vertices are 1.9e-6 mm from
571:         // the true CAD surfaces; refitting takes them to 2.0000.)
572:         refit_in_place(mesh, &mut hyp, config);
573:         out.push(hyp);
574:     }
575:     (out, leftovers)
576: }
577: 
578: 
579: /// Refit a committed hypothesis to its current face set, keeping the existing
580: /// fit if the fitter cannot produce one.
581: fn refit_in_place(mesh: &ConnectedMesh, hyp: &mut CompeteHyp, config: &Config) {
582:     let faces = hyp.faces().to_vec();
583:     let verts = vertices_of(mesh, &faces);
584:     let v = config.verbosity;
585:     match hyp {
586:         CompeteHyp::Planar(h) => {
587:             if let Some(fit) = PlanarSurface::fit_surface(mesh, &faces, &verts, v) {
588:                 *h = PlanarSurface::make_hypothesis(mesh, faces, verts, &fit);
589:             }
590:         }
591:         CompeteHyp::Conical(h) => {
592:             if let Some(mut fit) = ConicalSurface::fit_surface(mesh, &faces, &verts, v) {
593:                 ConicalSurface::determine_convexity(mesh, &faces, &mut fit);
594:                 *h = ConicalSurface::make_hypothesis(mesh, faces, verts, &fit);
595:             }
596:         }
597:         CompeteHyp::Spherical(h) => {
598:             if let Some(mut fit) = SphericalSurface::fit_surface(mesh, &faces, &verts, v) {
599:                 SphericalSurface::determine_convexity(mesh, &faces, &mut fit);
600:                 *h = SphericalSurface::make_hypothesis(mesh, faces, verts, &fit);
601:             }
602:         }
603:         CompeteHyp::Toroidal(h) => {
604:             if let Some(mut fit) = ToroidalSurface::fit_surface(mesh, &faces, &verts, v) {
605:                 ToroidalSurface::determine_convexity(mesh, &faces, &mut fit);
606:                 *h = ToroidalSurface::make_hypothesis(mesh, faces, verts, &fit);
607:             }
608:         }
609:     }
610: }
611: 
612: /// Grow a hypothesis of type `S` from `seed`: condition an initial fit on a
613: /// jet-similar seed patch, then flood-accept every region face the fit
614: /// validates and refit on the grown support, to a fixpoint. Returns the
615: /// hypothesis, its validated area, and its face list.
616: fn grow<S: SurfaceType>(
617:     mesh: &ConnectedMesh,
618:     in_region: &[bool],
619:     seed: usize,
620:     jet: &[JetField],
621:     centroids: &[[f64; 3]],
622:     config: &Config,
623:     min_faces: usize,
624: ) -> Option<(S::Hypothesis, f64, Vec<usize>)> {
625:     let patch = seed_patch(mesh, in_region, seed, jet);
626:     grow_from::<S>(mesh, in_region, seed, patch, centroids, config, min_faces)
627: }
628: 
629: /// The grow loop proper, from an explicit initial fit patch and flood start:
630: /// fit the patch, flood-accept every region face the fit validates, refit on
631: /// the grown support, to a fixpoint. Returns the hypothesis, its validated
632: /// area, and its face list.
633: fn grow_from<S: SurfaceType>(
634:     mesh: &ConnectedMesh,
635:     in_region: &[bool],
636:     start: usize,
637:     initial: Vec<usize>,
638:     centroids: &[[f64; 3]],
639:     config: &Config,
640:     min_faces: usize,
641: ) -> Option<(S::Hypothesis, f64, Vec<usize>)> {
642:     let stol = config.surface_tolerance_mm;
643:     let vtol = config.vertex_tolerance_mm;
644:     let mut accepted =
645:         condition_patch::<S>(mesh, start, initial, centroids, config, vtol, stol, min_faces);
646:     let mut prev = 0usize;
647:     for round in 0..MAX_GROW_ROUNDS {
648:         let verts = vertices_of(mesh, &accepted);
649:         let mut fit = S::fit_surface(mesh, &accepted, &verts, config.verbosity)?;
650:         S::determine_convexity(mesh, &accepted, &mut fit);
651:         let comp = flood::<S>(mesh, in_region, start, &fit, centroids, vtol, stol);
652:         if comp.is_empty() {
653:             return None; // the start face does not lie on its own fit — reject
654:         }
655:         if comp.len() == accepted.len() && round > 0 {
656:             accepted = comp;
657:             break;
658:         }
659:         if comp.len() == prev {
660:             // Oscillating between two sizes — stop at the larger.
661:             if comp.len() >= accepted.len() {
662:                 accepted = comp;
663:             }
664:             break;
665:         }
666:         prev = accepted.len();
667:         accepted = comp;
668:     }
669: 
670:     // Final fit + validated flood.
671:     let verts = vertices_of(mesh, &accepted);
672:     let mut fit = S::fit_surface(mesh, &accepted, &verts, config.verbosity)?;
673:     S::determine_convexity(mesh, &accepted, &mut fit);
674:     let faces = flood::<S>(mesh, in_region, start, &fit, centroids, vtol, stol);
675:     if faces.len() < min_faces {
676:         return None;
677:     }
678:     let area: f64 = faces.iter().map(|&fi| face_area(&mesh.faces[fi], &mesh.vertices)).sum();
679:     let verts = vertices_of(mesh, &faces);
680:     let hyp = S::make_hypothesis(mesh, faces.clone(), verts, &fit);
681:     Some((hyp, area, faces))
682: }
683: 
684: 
685: /// A committed torus's minor radius may not exceed this fraction of its major
686: /// radius. Real fillets and bend tubes are slim (r/R ≤ 0.5 across the corpus:
687: /// full donut 0.3, pipe-elbow 0.5, fillets 0.15–0.2); an r ≈ R "fat" torus is
688: /// a bogus fit a flat cap or wall band accepts loosely and then wins on area
689: /// (filleted_pipe: an r/R = 0.82 torus ate an annular end cap).
690: const MAX_TORUS_RADIUS_RATIO: f64 = 0.7;
691: 
692: /// How far a support sweeps around an axis, in radians: the angular extent of
693: /// `verts` about the line through `origin` along `axis`, taken as 2π minus the
694: /// largest angular gap between consecutive vertices, so a patch straddling the
695: /// angle origin measures correctly.
696: ///
697: /// This is the observable that says whether a rotational parameter is
698: /// determined by the data at all. A surface of revolution fitted to a patch
699: /// that never goes around its axis has its radius free, and a free parameter is
700: /// worse than useless in competition: the fit becomes a super-model of the
701: /// simpler surface it should have lost to — covering everything that surface
702: /// covers, then flooding a little further on the slack — so it takes the
703: /// largest-validated-area contest every time. See `MIN_TORUS_SWEEP_DEG` and
704: /// `MIN_CYLINDER_SWEEP_DEG`.
705: fn axial_sweep(
706:     mesh: &ConnectedMesh,
707:     verts: &[usize],
708:     origin: &[f64; 3],
709:     axis: &[f64; 3],
710: ) -> f64 {
711:     let (u, w) = super::perpendicular_basis(axis);
712:     let mut angles: Vec<f64> = verts
713:         .iter()
714:         .filter_map(|&vi| {
715:             let v = &mesh.vertices[vi];
716:             let d = [v.x - origin[0], v.y - origin[1], v.z - origin[2]];
717:             let (x, y) = (
718:                 d[0] * u[0] + d[1] * u[1] + d[2] * u[2],
719:                 d[0] * w[0] + d[1] * w[1] + d[2] * w[2],
720:             );
721:             // A vertex on the axis has no meaningful angle.
722:             (x.hypot(y) > 1e-12).then(|| y.atan2(x))
723:         })
724:         .collect();
725:     if angles.len() < 2 {
726:         return 0.0;
727:     }
728:     angles.sort_by(|p, q| p.partial_cmp(q).unwrap());
729:     let mut gap = angles[0] + std::f64::consts::TAU - angles[angles.len() - 1];
730:     for pair in angles.windows(2) {
731:         gap = gap.max(pair[1] - pair[0]);
732:     }
733:     (std::f64::consts::TAU - gap).max(0.0)
734: }
735: 
736: /// Sweep of a candidate's support about the axis whose radius it determines.
737: /// Planes and spheres have no axis — a sphere's radius is pinned by any patch —
738: /// so they report a full turn and are never rejected on this ground.
739: fn candidate_sweep(mesh: &ConnectedMesh, hyp: &CompeteHyp) -> f64 {
740:     match hyp {
741:         CompeteHyp::Toroidal(h) => torus_sweep(mesh, h),
742:         CompeteHyp::Conical(h) => conical_sweep(mesh, h),
743:         CompeteHyp::Planar(_) | CompeteHyp::Spherical(_) => std::f64::consts::TAU,
744:     }
745: }
746: 
747: /// Sweep of a toroidal hypothesis's support about its own axis.
748: fn torus_sweep(mesh: &ConnectedMesh, hyp: &ToroidalHypothesis) -> f64 {
749:     axial_sweep(mesh, &hyp.vertices, &hyp.center, &hyp.axis_direction)
750: }
751: 
752: /// Sweep of a cylinder's support about its own axis. Meaningless for a true
753: /// cone, whose apex pins the axis regardless of sweep, so cones report a full
754: /// turn.
755: fn conical_sweep(mesh: &ConnectedMesh, hyp: &ConicalHypothesis) -> f64 {
756:     if !hyp.is_cylinder() {
757:         return std::f64::consts::TAU;
758:     }
759:     axial_sweep(mesh, &hyp.vertices, &hyp.apex, &hyp.axis_direction)
760: }
761: 
762: /// Grow a toroidal candidate from an initial fit patch, trying flood starts
763: /// in order until one grows. Rejects degenerate giant-major-radius fits: a
764: /// short segment of straight cylinder fits a torus with an absurdly distant
765: /// axis (observed R ≈ 1500–3600 mm on ~100 mm models under the old BFS), and
766: /// a legitimate torus's major radius cannot exceed the mesh extent — the
767: /// same guard as the ladder's acceptance test. Also rejects "fat" tori
768: /// (`r > MAX_TORUS_RADIUS_RATIO·R`). The sweep guard that rejects a torus too
769: /// straight to have a major radius at all is applied to every candidate type
770: /// alike, in `compete_region` (see `MIN_AXIAL_SWEEP_DEG`).
771: fn torus_candidate(
772:     mesh: &ConnectedMesh,
773:     in_region: &[bool],
774:     initial: &[usize],
775:     starts: &[usize],
776:     centroids: &[[f64; 3]],
777:     config: &Config,
778: ) -> Option<(ToroidalHypothesis, f64, Vec<usize>)> {
779:     let bb_diag = bounding_box_diagonal(&mesh.vertices);
780:     for &start in starts.iter().take(MAX_TORUS_STARTS) {
781:         if let Some((hyp, area, faces)) = grow_from::<ToroidalSurface>(
782:             mesh,
783:             in_region,
784:             start,
785:             initial.to_vec(),
786:             centroids,
787:             config,
788:             MIN_TORUS_FACES,
789:         ) {
790:             // A grow that succeeded but produced an implausible torus says the
791:             // *type* is wrong here, so stop — retrying other flood starts from
792:             // the same fit patch would only rediscover it. Extra starts are
793:             // for the case where the grow itself failed.
794:             return (hyp.major_radius <= bb_diag
795:                 && hyp.minor_radius <= MAX_TORUS_RADIUS_RATIO * hyp.major_radius)
796:             .then_some((hyp, area, faces));
797:         }
798:     }
799:     None
800: }
801: 
802: /// Stage 2.3(c) bent-tube promotion. A cylinder flood that stalled against
803: /// region faces whose jet continues the tube — κ1 ≈ the tube's 1/r, |κ2|
804: /// well below κ1 (the same tube swinging around a bend, not a cap plane's
805: /// (0,0) or an end sphere's (κ,κ)) — is the developable prefix of a bent
806: /// tube. Fit a torus to the pure-bend patch just past the stall line
807: /// (frontier faces plus their off-cylinder tube-jet neighbours) and grow it;
808: /// the result competes on validated area like any other candidate.
809: ///
810: /// The cylinder fit itself locates the tangency — frontier faces failed
811: /// cylinder validation — so unlike a jet-class seed patch (which cannot tell
812: /// bend from straight tube: both read the same κ1) this patch cannot
813: /// straddle the junction.
814: fn promote_bend(
815:     mesh: &ConnectedMesh,
816:     in_region: &[bool],
817:     cyl: &ConicalHypothesis,
818:     cyl_faces: &[usize],
819:     jet: &[JetField],
820:     centroids: &[[f64; 3]],
821:     config: &Config,
822: ) -> Option<(ToroidalHypothesis, f64, Vec<usize>)> {
823:     if cyl.radius_at_apex <= 0.0 {
824:         return None;
825:     }
826:     let k_tube = 1.0 / cyl.radius_at_apex;
827:     let grow_vtol = config.vertex_tolerance_mm;
828: 
829:     // Max vertex distance to the stalled cylinder — the "past the tangency"
830:     // test (frontier faces failed the flood at exactly this tolerance).
831:     let off_cyl_dist = |fi: usize| -> f64 {
832:         let face = &mesh.faces[fi];
833:         let vc = face.vertex_count as usize;
834:         face.vertex_indices[..vc]
835:             .iter()
836:             .map(|&vi| {
837:                 super::cylindrical::vertex_to_cylinder_distance(
838:                     &mesh.vertices[vi],
839:                     &cyl.apex,
840:                     &cyl.axis_direction,
841:                     cyl.radius_at_apex,
842:                 )
843:                 .abs()
844:             })
845:             .fold(0.0_f64, f64::max)
846:     };
847:     // Tube-continuation jet signature. The κ2 bound is generous: a bend's
848:     // inner (saddle) side reads |κ2| = 1/(R−r), which approaches κ1 = 1/r
849:     // only for very tight bends; junction rows have contaminated jets and
850:     // are excluded from the fit patch either way (the flood claims them).
851:     let tube_jet = |fi: usize| -> bool {
852:         let a1 = jet[fi].k1.abs();
853:         a1 >= 0.5 * k_tube && a1 <= 2.0 * k_tube && jet[fi].k2.abs() <= 0.8 * a1
854:     };
855: 
856:     // Frontier: in-region neighbours of the cylinder support that are past
857:     // the tangency and continue the tube.
858:     let mut seen: HashSet<usize> = cyl_faces.iter().copied().collect();
859:     let mut frontier: Vec<usize> = Vec::new();
860:     for &fi in cyl_faces {
861:         let vc = mesh.faces[fi].vertex_count as usize;
862:         for &ni in &mesh.faces[fi].neighbors[..vc] {
863:             if ni < 0 {
864:                 continue;
865:             }
866:             let ni = ni as usize;
867:             if !in_region[ni] || !seen.insert(ni) {
868:                 continue;
869:             }
870:             if off_cyl_dist(ni) > grow_vtol && tube_jet(ni) {
871:                 frontier.push(ni);
872:             }
873:         }
874:     }
875:     if frontier.is_empty() {
876:         return None; // finished a straight pipe (or stalled at a cap/sphere)
877:     }
878:     frontier.sort_unstable();
879: 
880:     // Pure-bend fit patch: depth-limited multi-source BFS from the frontier
881:     // through off-cylinder tube-jet faces. (Neighbours examined and rejected
882:     // above stay excluded — junction rows with contaminated jets would only
883:     // pollute the fit.)
884:     let mut patch: Vec<usize> = frontier.clone();
885:     let mut ring = frontier.clone();
886:     for _depth in 0..BEND_PATCH_DEPTH {
887:         if patch.len() >= BEND_PATCH_CAP {
888:             break;
889:         }
890:         let mut next: Vec<usize> = Vec::new();
891:         for &fi in &ring {
892:             let vc = mesh.faces[fi].vertex_count as usize;
893:             for &ni in &mesh.faces[fi].neighbors[..vc] {
894:                 if ni < 0 {
895:                     continue;
896:                 }
897:                 let ni = ni as usize;
898:                 if !in_region[ni] || seen.contains(&ni) {
899:                     continue;
900:                 }
901:                 if off_cyl_dist(ni) <= grow_vtol || !tube_jet(ni) {
902:                     continue;
903:                 }
904:                 seen.insert(ni);
905:                 patch.push(ni);
906:                 next.push(ni);
907:                 if patch.len() >= BEND_PATCH_CAP {
908:                     break;
909:                 }
910:             }
911:         }
912:         if next.is_empty() {
913:             break;
914:         }
915:         ring = next;
916:     }
917:     patch.sort_unstable();
918: 
919:     // Flood starts: deepest-off-cylinder first — the faces most surely
920:     // interior to the bend (frontier faces sit on the junction row and may
921:     // not validate the torus fit themselves).
922:     let mut starts = patch.clone();
923:     starts.sort_by(|&a, &b| {
924:         off_cyl_dist(b)
925:             .partial_cmp(&off_cyl_dist(a))
926:             .unwrap()
927:             .then_with(|| a.cmp(&b))
928:     });
929: 
930:     torus_candidate(mesh, in_region, &patch, &starts, centroids, config)
931: }
932: 
933: /// Connected region-faces reachable from `seed` that all validate `fit`
934: /// (vertex tolerance + centroid tolerance + orientation coherence).
935: fn flood<S: SurfaceType>(
936:     mesh: &ConnectedMesh,
937:     in_region: &[bool],
938:     seed: usize,
939:     fit: &S::Fit,
940:     centroids: &[[f64; 3]],
941:     vtol: f64,
942:     stol: f64,
943: ) -> Vec<usize> {
944:     if !face_validates::<S>(mesh, seed, fit, centroids, vtol, stol) {
945:         return Vec::new();
946:     }
947:     let mut seen: HashSet<usize> = HashSet::new();
948:     seen.insert(seed);
949:     let mut out = Vec::new();
950:     let mut stack = vec![seed];
951:     while let Some(fi) = stack.pop() {
952:         out.push(fi);
953:         let vc = mesh.faces[fi].vertex_count as usize;
954:         for &ni in &mesh.faces[fi].neighbors[..vc] {
955:             if ni < 0 {
956:                 continue;
957:             }
958:             let ni = ni as usize;
959:             if in_region[ni]
960:                 && !seen.contains(&ni)
961:                 && face_validates::<S>(mesh, ni, fit, centroids, vtol, stol)
962:             {
963:                 seen.insert(ni);
964:                 stack.push(ni);
965:             }
966:         }
967:     }
968:     out.sort_unstable();
969:     out
970: }
971: 
972: /// Does face `fi` validate `fit` (all vertices ≤ vtol, centroid ≤ stol,
973: /// orientation coherent)?
974: fn face_validates<S: SurfaceType>(
975:     mesh: &ConnectedMesh,
976:     fi: usize,
977:     fit: &S::Fit,
978:     centroids: &[[f64; 3]],
979:     vtol: f64,
980:     stol: f64,
981: ) -> bool {
982:     if !S::convexity_check(fit, mesh, fi) {
983:         return false;
984:     }
985:     let face = &mesh.faces[fi];
986:     let vc = face.vertex_count as usize;
987:     for &vi in &face.vertex_indices[..vc] {
988:         let v = &mesh.vertices[vi];
989:         if S::vertex_distance(fit, &[v.x, v.y, v.z]) > vtol {
990:             return false;
991:         }
992:     }
993:     S::centroid_distance(fit, &centroids[fi]) <= stol
994: }
995: 
996: /// Max distance from any vertex of face `fi` to `fit` — the trim criterion for
997: /// conditioning the seed patch (`condition_patch`).
998: fn face_max_vertex_dist<S: SurfaceType>(mesh: &ConnectedMesh, fi: usize, fit: &S::Fit) -> f64 {
999:     let face = &mesh.faces[fi];
1000:     let vc = face.vertex_count as usize;
1001:     face.vertex_indices[..vc]
1002:         .iter()
1003:         .map(|&vi| {
1004:             let v = &mesh.vertices[vi];
1005:             S::vertex_distance(fit, &[v.x, v.y, v.z])
1006:         })
1007:         .fold(0.0_f64, f64::max)
1008: }
1009: 
1010: /// Condition the seed patch so the seed lies on the patch's own fit. A
1011: /// depth-limited jet-similar patch can bleed a row or two across a tangent
1012: /// junction between two surfaces of the same curvature class — a short
1013: /// cylinder wall into its fillet, where the transition faces' κ1 ramps from
1014: /// the wall's toward the fillet's — which pulls the fit off far enough that
1015: /// the seed fails its own fit and the grow gives up (leaving the wall to be
1016: /// eaten by an over-grown torus). Trim the worst-fitting patch face (never the
1017: /// seed) and refit until the seed validates, bottoming out at a size floor so
1018: /// the fit stays well-determined. Fit-validated, in the spirit of "validation
1019: /// decides geometry"; a no-op when the patch is already clean.
1020: ///
1021: /// When the patch admits no fit of this type at all there is no residual to
1022: /// rank by, so the round instead drops the face geometrically farthest from
1023: /// the seed: a straddling patch is unfittable precisely because its far end
1024: /// sits on another surface, and shrinking toward the seed is the one move that
1025: /// is monotonically local.
1026: #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
1027: fn condition_patch<S: SurfaceType>(
1028:     mesh: &ConnectedMesh,
1029:     seed: usize,
1030:     mut patch: Vec<usize>,
1031:     centroids: &[[f64; 3]],
1032:     config: &Config,
1033:     vtol: f64,
1034:     stol: f64,
1035:     min_faces: usize,
1036: ) -> Vec<usize> {
1037:     let floor = min_faces.max(MIN_CONDITION_FACES);
1038:     for _ in 0..PATCH_TRIM_ROUNDS {
1039:         if patch.len() <= floor {
1040:             break;
1041:         }
1042:         let verts = vertices_of(mesh, &patch);
1043:         let mut fit = match S::fit_surface(mesh, &patch, &verts, config.verbosity) {
1044:             Some(f) => f,
1045:             None => {
1046:                 // No fit to rank by — shrink toward the seed instead.
1047:                 let far = patch
1048:                     .iter()
1049:                     .copied()
1050:                     .filter(|&f| f != seed)
1051:                     .max_by(|&a, &b| {
1052:                         centroid_gap(centroids, seed, a)
1053:                             .partial_cmp(&centroid_gap(centroids, seed, b))
1054:                             .unwrap()
1055:                     });
1056:                 match far {
1057:                     Some(f) => {
1058:                         patch.retain(|&p| p != f);
1059:                         continue;
1060:                     }
1061:                     None => break,
1062:                 }
1063:             }
1064:         };
1065:         S::determine_convexity(mesh, &patch, &mut fit);
1066:         if face_validates::<S>(mesh, seed, &fit, centroids, vtol, stol) {
1067:             break;
1068:         }
1069:         let worst = patch
1070:             .iter()
1071:             .copied()
1072:             .filter(|&f| f != seed)
1073:             .max_by(|&a, &b| {
1074:                 face_max_vertex_dist::<S>(mesh, a, &fit)
1075:                     .partial_cmp(&face_max_vertex_dist::<S>(mesh, b, &fit))
1076:                     .unwrap()
1077:             });
1078:         match worst {
1079:             Some(w) => patch.retain(|&f| f != w),
1080:             None => break,
1081:         }
1082:     }
1083:     patch
1084: }
1085: 
1086: /// Distance between two faces' centroids.
1087: fn centroid_gap(centroids: &[[f64; 3]], a: usize, b: usize) -> f64 {
1088:     let (p, q) = (centroids[a], centroids[b]);
1089:     ((p[0] - q[0]).powi(2) + (p[1] - q[1]).powi(2) + (p[2] - q[2]).powi(2)).sqrt()
1090: }
1091: 
1092: /// A small patch around `seed`, capped and depth-limited, for a well-conditioned
1093: /// initial fit: jet-similar faces (same curvature class, magnitude within 2×,
1094: /// and within `PATCH_RADII` radii of curvature) where the jet is informative.
1095: ///
1096: /// Falls back to the plain topological neighbourhood when that leaves too
1097: /// little to fit. The jet is a *prior*, not a gate — validation decides every
1098: /// face — and on a small fillet tessellated into a handful of facets it is
1099: /// simply not reliable enough to gate on: measured on
1100: /// `stepped_block_toroidal_fillets` medium, adjacent facets of one exact
1101: /// r = 0.5 arc read κ₁ = 0.023, 0.162 and 2.2 (radii of 43 mm, 6 mm and
1102: /// 0.45 mm for the same 0.5 mm fillet). The similarity filter then admits 2–4
1103: /// faces, too few to determine a 5-DOF cylinder, so every candidate fails and
1104: /// the arc degrades to one plane per facet. Handing the fit an unfiltered
1105: /// neighbourhood instead costs nothing in soundness: a patch straddling a
1106: /// junction is what `condition_patch` trims and what flood validation rejects.
1107: fn seed_patch(mesh: &ConnectedMesh, in_region: &[bool], seed: usize, jet: &[JetField]) -> Vec<usize> {
1108:     let patch = grow_patch(mesh, in_region, seed, jet, true);
1109:     if patch.len() >= MIN_CONDITION_FACES {
1110:         return patch;
1111:     }
1112:     grow_patch(mesh, in_region, seed, jet, false)
1113: }
1114: 
1115: /// Depth-limited BFS around `seed`, optionally filtered by jet similarity.
1116: fn grow_patch(
1117:     mesh: &ConnectedMesh,
1118:     in_region: &[bool],
1119:     seed: usize,
1120:     jet: &[JetField],
1121:     use_jet: bool,
1122: ) -> Vec<usize> {
1123:     let seed_class = curvature_class(jet[seed]);
1124:     let seed_mag = jet[seed].k1.abs().max(jet[seed].k2.abs());
1125:     let seed_c = super::face_centroid(&mesh.faces[seed], &mesh.vertices);
1126:     let radius_limit = if use_jet && seed_mag > 1e-9 {
1127:         PATCH_RADII / seed_mag
1128:     } else {
1129:         f64::INFINITY
1130:     };
1131:     let mut seen: HashSet<usize> = HashSet::new();
1132:     seen.insert(seed);
1133:     let mut out = vec![seed];
1134:     // Depth-limited BFS (frontier by ring).
1135:     let mut frontier = vec![seed];
1136:     for _depth in 0..MAX_PATCH_DEPTH {
1137:         if out.len() >= SEED_PATCH_CAP {
1138:             break;
1139:         }
1140:         let mut next = Vec::new();
1141:         for &fi in &frontier {
1142:             let vc = mesh.faces[fi].vertex_count as usize;
1143:             for &ni in &mesh.faces[fi].neighbors[..vc] {
1144:                 if ni < 0 {
1145:                     continue;
1146:                 }
1147:                 let ni = ni as usize;
1148:                 if !in_region[ni] || seen.contains(&ni) {
1149:                     continue;
1150:                 }
1151:                 if use_jet {
1152:                     if curvature_class(jet[ni]) != seed_class {
1153:                         continue;
1154:                     }
1155:                     let mag = jet[ni].k1.abs().max(jet[ni].k2.abs());
1156:                     if seed_mag > 1e-9 && (mag > 1.5 * seed_mag || mag < 0.667 * seed_mag) {
1157:                         continue;
1158:                     }
1159:                     let c = super::face_centroid(&mesh.faces[ni], &mesh.vertices);
1160:                     let d = ((c[0] - seed_c[0]).powi(2)
1161:                         + (c[1] - seed_c[1]).powi(2)
1162:                         + (c[2] - seed_c[2]).powi(2))
1163:                     .sqrt();
1164:                     if d > radius_limit {
1165:                         continue;
1166:                     }
1167:                 }
1168:                 seen.insert(ni);
1169:                 out.push(ni);
1170:                 next.push(ni);
1171:                 if out.len() >= SEED_PATCH_CAP {
1172:                     break;
1173:                 }
1174:             }
1175:         }
1176:         frontier = next;
1177:     }
1178:     out.sort_unstable();
1179:     out
1180: }
1181: 
1182: #[derive(PartialEq, Eq, Clone, Copy, Debug)]
1183: enum CurvClass {
1184:     Flat,
1185:     Developable,
1186:     Spherelike,
1187: }
1188: 
1189: fn curvature_class(jf: JetField) -> CurvClass {
1190:     // Magnitude-ordered so a concave feature (dominant curvature negative, in
1191:     // k2) is not misread — see the note in `compete_region`.
1192:     let a1 = jf.k1.abs().max(jf.k2.abs());
1193:     let a2 = jf.k1.abs().min(jf.k2.abs());
1194:     // Absolute floor is generous; only used to cluster the seed patch.
1195:     if a1 < 1e-4 {
1196:         CurvClass::Flat
1197:     } else if a2 >= 0.4 * a1 {
1198:         CurvClass::Spherelike
1199:     } else {
1200:         CurvClass::Developable
1201:     }
1202: }
1203: 
1204: /// Curvature mismatch of a face vs its region neighbors (low = interior).
1205: fn neighbor_jet_mismatch(mesh: &ConnectedMesh, fi: usize, jet: &[JetField], in_region: &[bool]) -> f64 {
1206:     let jf = jet[fi];
1207:     let vc = mesh.faces[fi].vertex_count as usize;
1208:     let mut worst = 0.0_f64;
1209:     for &ni in &mesh.faces[fi].neighbors[..vc] {
1210:         if ni < 0 {
1211:             continue;
1212:         }
1213:         let ni = ni as usize;
1214:         if !in_region[ni] {
1215:             continue;
1216:         }
1217:         let jn = jet[ni];
1218:         let d = (jf.k1 - jn.k1).abs() + (jf.k2 - jn.k2).abs();
1219:         worst = worst.max(d);
1220:     }
1221:     worst
1222: }
1223: 
1224: /// Max distance from face `fi`'s vertices to a committed hypothesis.

1225: fn max_vertex_dist(mesh: &ConnectedMesh, hyp: &CompeteHyp, fi: usize) -> f64 {
1226:     let face = &mesh.faces[fi];
1227:     let vc = face.vertex_count as usize;
1228:     face.vertex_indices[..vc]

1278: /// Largest bounding-box dimension of a region's vertices.
1279: fn region_extent(mesh: &ConnectedMesh, region: &Region) -> f64 {
1280:     let mut min = [f64::INFINITY; 3];
1281:     let mut max = [f64::NEG_INFINITY; 3];
1282:     for &vi in &region.vertices {
1283:         let v = &mesh.vertices[vi];
1284:         for (k, c) in [v.x, v.y, v.z].into_iter().enumerate() {
1285:             min[k] = min[k].min(c);
1286:             max[k] = max[k].max(c);
1287:         }
1288:     }
1289:     (max[0] - min[0]).max(max[1] - min[1]).max(max[2] - min[2])
1290: }

1: //! The `SurfaceType` trait: the shared abstraction over the analytic surface
2: //! fitters (planar, conical, spherical, toroidal).
3: //!
4: //! Each fitter implements `fit_surface` + the validation/convexity/hypothesis
5: //! methods; the stage-2.3 whole-region ladder (`classify.rs`) and scoped
6: //! competition (`compete.rs`) drive them generically via this trait. The
7: //! former BFS hypothesis-generation engine that also used this trait was
8: //! removed with the stage-2 rewrite (see DEVELOPMENT_PLAN Rewrite Roadmap
9: //! step 7).
10: 
11: use crate::stage1::ConnectedMesh;
12: 
13: pub(super) trait SurfaceType {
14:     /// The fitted surface parameters (e.g., axis, radius, center).
15:     type Fit: Clone;
16:     /// The output hypothesis struct.
17:     type Hypothesis;
18: 
19:     /// Fit a surface to the given faces and vertices.
20:     /// Returns None if fitting fails.
21:     fn fit_surface(
22:         mesh: &ConnectedMesh,
23:         face_list: &[usize],
24:         vertex_list: &[usize],
25:         verbosity: u8,
26:     ) -> Option<Self::Fit>;
27: 
28:     /// Compute the absolute distance from a vertex position to the fitted surface.
29:     fn vertex_distance(fit: &Self::Fit, pos: &[f64; 3]) -> f64;
30: 
31:     /// Compute the absolute distance from a centroid to the fitted surface.
32:     /// Default: same as vertex_distance.
33:     fn centroid_distance(fit: &Self::Fit, centroid: &[f64; 3]) -> f64 {
34:         Self::vertex_distance(fit, centroid)
35:     }
36: 
37:     /// Check convexity consistency: does `fi` match the convexity of the fit?
38:     /// Return true to accept, false to reject.
39:     /// Default implementation always accepts (e.g., for planar surfaces).
40:     fn convexity_check(_fit: &Self::Fit, _mesh: &ConnectedMesh, _fi: usize) -> bool {
41:         true
42:     }
43: 
44:     /// Determine convexity from a set of faces and update the fit.
45:     /// Default: no-op (for surface types without convexity).
46:     fn determine_convexity(_mesh: &ConnectedMesh, _face_list: &[usize], _fit: &mut Self::Fit) {}
47: 
48:     /// Construct the final hypothesis from the fitted surface and its faces.
49:     fn make_hypothesis(
50:         mesh: &ConnectedMesh,
51:         faces: Vec<usize>,
52:         vertices: Vec<usize>,
53:         fit: &Self::Fit,
54:     ) -> Self::Hypothesis;
55: }

1: //! Stage 2 cylindrical fitting utilities.
2: //! Core fitting functions used by ConicalSurface (in conical.rs) for cylinder fitting.
3: 
4: 
5: use levenberg_marquardt::{LeastSquaresProblem, LevenbergMarquardt};
6: use nalgebra::{Const, Dyn, OMatrix, OVector, Owned};
7: 
8: use crate::stage1::{MeshFace, MeshVertex};
9: 
10: use super::{
11:     build_normal_covariance, cross3,
12:     normalize3, perpendicular_basis, smallest_eigenvector_3x3,
13: };
14: 
15: // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
16: // Cylindrical fitting helpers
17: // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
18: 
19: /// Compute the distance from a vertex to a cylinder surface.
20: /// Returns the signed distance: positive if outside the cylinder, negative if inside.
21: pub(super) fn vertex_to_cylinder_distance(
22:     v: &MeshVertex,
23:     axis_origin: &[f64; 3],
24:     axis_direction: &[f64; 3],
25:     radius: f64,
26: ) -> f64 {
27:     // Vector from axis origin to vertex
28:     let dx = v.x - axis_origin[0];
29:     let dy = v.y - axis_origin[1];
30:     let dz = v.z - axis_origin[2];
31:     // Project onto axis
32:     let t = dx * axis_direction[0] + dy * axis_direction[1] + dz * axis_direction[2];
33:     // Radial vector (perpendicular to axis)
34:     let rx = dx - t * axis_direction[0];
35:     let ry = dy - t * axis_direction[1];
36:     let rz = dz - t * axis_direction[2];
37:     let radial_dist = (rx * rx + ry * ry + rz * rz).sqrt();
38:     radial_dist - radius
39: }
40: 
41: /// Given an axis direction, project vertices onto the perpendicular plane
42: /// and fit a 2D circle. Returns (axis_origin_3d, radius) or None if degenerate.
43: pub(super) fn fit_circle_for_axis(
44:     axis_dir: &[f64; 3],
45:     vertex_set: &[usize],
46:     vertices: &[MeshVertex],
47: ) -> Option<([f64; 3], f64)> {
48:     let (u, w) = perpendicular_basis(axis_dir);
49:     let verts: Vec<usize> = vertex_set.to_vec();
50:     let n = verts.len();
51:     if n < 3 {
52:         return None;
53:     }
54: 
55:     let mut xs = Vec::with_capacity(n);
56:     let mut ys = Vec::with_capacity(n);
57:     for &vi in &verts {
58:         let v = &vertices[vi];
59:         xs.push(v.x * u[0] + v.y * u[1] + v.z * u[2]);
60:         ys.push(v.x * w[0] + v.y * w[1] + v.z * w[2]);
61:     }
62: 
63:     // Center the 2D data to improve numerical conditioning.
64:     let nf = n as f64;
65:     let mean_x = xs.iter().sum::<f64>() / nf;
66:     let mean_y = ys.iter().sum::<f64>() / nf;
67:     for i in 0..n {
68:         xs[i] -= mean_x;
69:         ys[i] -= mean_y;
70:     }
71: 
72:     // Algebraic circle fit: x² + y² + Dx + Ey + F = 0
73:     let mut sx = 0.0_f64;
74:     let mut sy = 0.0_f64;
75:     let mut sx2 = 0.0_f64;
76:     let mut sy2 = 0.0_f64;
77:     let mut sxy = 0.0_f64;
78:     let mut sx3 = 0.0_f64;
79:     let mut sy3 = 0.0_f64;
80:     let mut sx2y = 0.0_f64;
81:     let mut sxy2 = 0.0_f64;
82: 
83:     for i in 0..n {
84:         let x = xs[i];
85:         let y = ys[i];
86:         sx += x;
87:         sy += y;
88:         sx2 += x * x;
89:         sy2 += y * y;
90:         sxy += x * y;
91:         sx3 += x * x * x;
92:         sy3 += y * y * y;
93:         sx2y += x * x * y;
94:         sxy2 += x * y * y;
95:     }
96: 
97:     let rhs0 = -(sx3 + sxy2);
98:     let rhs1 = -(sx2y + sy3);
99:     let rhs2 = -(sx2 + sy2);
100: 
101:     let a = [[sx2, sxy, sx], [sxy, sy2, sy], [sx, sy, nf]];
102: 
103:     let det_a = a[0][0] * (a[1][1] * a[2][2] - a[1][2] * a[2][1])
104:               - a[0][1] * (a[1][0] * a[2][2] - a[1][2] * a[2][0])
105:               + a[0][2] * (a[1][0] * a[2][1] - a[1][1] * a[2][0]);
106: 
107:     if det_a.abs() < 1e-30 {
108:         return None;
109:     }
110: 
111:     let inv_det = 1.0 / det_a;
112: 
113:     let d_val = inv_det * (rhs0 * (a[1][1] * a[2][2] - a[1][2] * a[2][1])
114:                          - a[0][1] * (rhs1 * a[2][2] - a[1][2] * rhs2)
115:                          + a[0][2] * (rhs1 * a[2][1] - a[1][1] * rhs2));
116: 
117:     let e_val = inv_det * (a[0][0] * (rhs1 * a[2][2] - a[1][2] * rhs2)
118:                          - rhs0 * (a[1][0] * a[2][2] - a[1][2] * a[2][0])
119:                          + a[0][2] * (a[1][0] * rhs2 - rhs1 * a[2][0]));
120: 
121:     let f_val = inv_det * (a[0][0] * (a[1][1] * rhs2 - rhs1 * a[2][1])
122:                          - a[0][1] * (a[1][0] * rhs2 - rhs1 * a[2][0])
123:                          + rhs0 * (a[1][0] * a[2][1] - a[1][1] * a[2][0]));
124: 
125:     let center_x = -d_val / 2.0;
126:     let center_y = -e_val / 2.0;
127:     let r_sq = center_x * center_x + center_y * center_y - f_val;
128:     if r_sq <= 0.0 {
129:         return None;
130:     }
131:     let radius = r_sq.sqrt();
132: 
133:     let abs_center_x = center_x + mean_x;
134:     let abs_center_y = center_y + mean_y;
135:     let axis_origin = [
136:         abs_center_x * u[0] + abs_center_y * w[0],
137:         abs_center_x * u[1] + abs_center_y * w[1],
138:         abs_center_x * u[2] + abs_center_y * w[2],
139:     ];
140: 
141:     Some((axis_origin, radius))
142: }
143: 
144: /// LM problem for cylinder fitting.
145: /// Parameters: [alpha, beta, qx, qy, qz, radius]
146: /// alpha, beta: tilt from initial axis in perpendicular directions
147: /// qx, qy, qz: axis point
148: /// radius: cylinder radius
149: struct CylinderLMProblem {
150:     points: Vec<[f64; 3]>,
151:     a0: [f64; 3],
152:     u0: [f64; 3],
153:     w0: [f64; 3],
154:     params: OVector<f64, Const<6>>,
155: }
156: 
157: impl CylinderLMProblem {
158:     fn new(
159:         points: Vec<[f64; 3]>,
160:         initial_axis: [f64; 3],
161:         initial_origin: [f64; 3],
162:         initial_radius: f64,
163:     ) -> Self {
164:         let (u0, w0) = perpendicular_basis(&initial_axis);
165:         let params = OVector::<f64, Const<6>>::new(
166:             0.0, 0.0,
167:             initial_origin[0], initial_origin[1], initial_origin[2],
168:             initial_radius,
169:         );
170:         Self { points, a0: initial_axis, u0, w0, params }
171:     }
172: 
173:     fn axis_dir_from_params(&self, params: &OVector<f64, Const<6>>) -> [f64; 3] {
174:         let (alpha, beta) = (params[0], params[1]);
175:         let mut v = [
176:             self.a0[0] + alpha * self.u0[0] + beta * self.w0[0],
177:             self.a0[1] + alpha * self.u0[1] + beta * self.w0[1],
178:             self.a0[2] + alpha * self.u0[2] + beta * self.w0[2],
179:         ];
180:         normalize3(&mut v);
181:         v
182:     }
183: 
184:     fn compute_residuals_for(&self, params: &OVector<f64, Const<6>>) -> OVector<f64, Dyn> {
185:         let a = self.axis_dir_from_params(params);
186:         let q = [params[2], params[3], params[4]];
187:         let r = params[5];
188:         let n = self.points.len();
189:         let mut residuals = OVector::<f64, Dyn>::zeros_generic(Dyn(n), Const::<1>);
190:         for (i, p) in self.points.iter().enumerate() {
191:             let d = [p[0] - q[0], p[1] - q[1], p[2] - q[2]];
192:             let c = cross3(&d, &a);
193:             let h = (c[0] * c[0] + c[1] * c[1] + c[2] * c[2]).sqrt();
194:             residuals[i] = h - r;
195:         }
196:         residuals
197:     }
198: }
199: 
200: impl LeastSquaresProblem<f64, Dyn, Const<6>> for CylinderLMProblem {
201:     type ParameterStorage = Owned<f64, Const<6>>;
202:     type ResidualStorage = Owned<f64, Dyn>;
203:     type JacobianStorage = Owned<f64, Dyn, Const<6>>;
204: 
205:     fn set_params(&mut self, x: &OVector<f64, Const<6>>) {
206:         self.params.copy_from(x);
207:     }
208: 
209:     fn params(&self) -> OVector<f64, Const<6>> {
210:         self.params
211:     }
212: 
213:     fn residuals(&self) -> Option<OVector<f64, Dyn>> {
214:         Some(self.compute_residuals_for(&self.params))
215:     }
216: 
217:     fn jacobian(&self) -> Option<OMatrix<f64, Dyn, Const<6>>> {
218:         let n = self.points.len();
219:         let eps = 1e-8;
220:         let mut jac = OMatrix::<f64, Dyn, Const<6>>::zeros_generic(
221:             Dyn(n), Const::<6>,
222:         );
223:         for j in 0..6 {
224:             let mut pp = self.params;
225:             pp[j] += eps;
226:             let rp = self.compute_residuals_for(&pp);
227:             let mut pm = self.params;
228:             pm[j] -= eps;
229:             let rm = self.compute_residuals_for(&pm);
230:             for i in 0..n {
231:                 jac[(i, j)] = (rp[i] - rm[i]) / (2.0 * eps);
232:             }
233:         }
234:         Some(jac)
235:     }
236: }
237: 
238: /// Fit cylinder parameters (axis direction, axis origin, radius) from a set of faces.
239: /// Tries two axis estimation strategies — normal-covariance (good for wide arcs) and
240: /// vertex-PCA (good for narrow arcs / drum-laced triangles) — picks the better one,
241: /// then refines the axis direction by minimizing vertex-to-cylinder SSE.
242: /// Returns (axis_origin, axis_direction, radius).
243: pub(super) fn fit_cylinder(
244:     face_indices: &[usize],
245:     vertex_set: &[usize],
246:     faces: &[MeshFace],
247:     vertices: &[MeshVertex],
248: ) -> Option<([f64; 3], [f64; 3], f64)> {
249:     if vertex_set.len() < 3 {
250:         return None;
251:     }
252: 
253:     // Axis from smallest eigenvector of area-weighted normal covariance.
254:     let cov = build_normal_covariance(face_indices, faces, vertices);
255:     let mut axis_dir = smallest_eigenvector_3x3(&cov);
256:     let len = normalize3(&mut axis_dir);
257:     if len < 1e-15 {
258:         return None;
259:     }
260: 
261:     // Initial circle fit to get starting origin and radius.
262:     let (axis_origin, radius) = fit_circle_for_axis(&axis_dir, vertex_set, vertices)?;
263: 
264:     // Recenter vertices around their centroid for numerical stability.
265:     let n_pts = vertex_set.len() as f64;
266:     let mut centroid = [0.0_f64; 3];
267:     for &vi in vertex_set.iter() {
268:         centroid[0] += vertices[vi].x;
269:         centroid[1] += vertices[vi].y;
270:         centroid[2] += vertices[vi].z;
271:     }
272:     centroid[0] /= n_pts;
273:     centroid[1] /= n_pts;
274:     centroid[2] /= n_pts;
275: 
276:     let points: Vec<[f64; 3]> = vertex_set.iter()
277:         .map(|&vi| [
278:             vertices[vi].x - centroid[0],
279:             vertices[vi].y - centroid[1],
280:             vertices[vi].z - centroid[2],
281:         ])
282:         .collect();
283:     let centered_origin = [
284:         axis_origin[0] - centroid[0],
285:         axis_origin[1] - centroid[1],
286:         axis_origin[2] - centroid[2],
287:     ];
288: 
289:     // Refine with Levenberg-Marquardt optimization on radial residuals.
290:     let problem = CylinderLMProblem::new(points, axis_dir, centered_origin, radius);
291:     let (result, _report) = LevenbergMarquardt::new().with_patience(150).minimize(problem);
292:     let refined_dir = result.axis_dir_from_params(&result.params);
293:     let refined_origin = [
294:         result.params[2] + centroid[0],
295:         result.params[3] + centroid[1],
296:         result.params[4] + centroid[2],
297:     ];
298:     let refined_radius = result.params[5];
299: 
300:     if refined_radius > 0.0 {
301:         Some((refined_origin, refined_dir, refined_radius))
302:     } else {
303:         Some((axis_origin, axis_dir, radius))
304:     }
305: }
306: 
307: // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
308: // Unit tests
309: // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
310: 
311: #[cfg(test)]
312: mod tests {
313:     use super::*;
314:     use std::f64::consts::PI;
315: 
316:     /// A partial arc — even a single row of quads across 90° — pins a cylinder
317:     /// exactly. Worth pinning down, because fillet cylinders across the corpus
318:     /// used to come out 1e-3 mm wrong (rounded_cube's r = 2 edge fillets read
319:     /// 1.9976–1.9984) and the fitter was the obvious suspect. It is not: the
320:     /// fits were stale, computed on the support the flood grew and never
321:     /// recomputed after claim resolution trimmed the junction rows back off
322:     /// (`refit_in_place` in compete.rs). If this test ever starts failing, the
323:     /// suspicion is warranted again.
324:     #[test]
325:     fn cylinder_fit_exact_on_partial_arc() {
326:         for (nrows, ncols) in [(7usize, 7usize), (2, 37), (2, 7), (3, 37), (2, 13)] {
327:             let r = 2.0_f64;
328:             let sweep = PI / 2.0;
329:             let mut vertices = Vec::new();
330:             for i in 0..nrows {
331:                 let h = 6.0 * i as f64 / (nrows - 1) as f64;
332:                 for j in 0..ncols {
333:                     let th = sweep * j as f64 / (ncols - 1) as f64;
334:                     vertices.push(MeshVertex::from_xyz(r * th.cos(), h, r * th.sin()));
335:                 }
336:             }
337:             let mut faces = Vec::new();
338:             for i in 0..nrows - 1 {
339:                 for j in 0..ncols - 1 {
340:                     let th = sweep * (j as f64 + 0.5) / (ncols - 1) as f64;
341:                     faces.push(MeshFace {
342:                         vertex_count: 4,
343:                         vertex_indices: [
344:                             i * ncols + j,
345:                             i * ncols + j + 1,
346:                             (i + 1) * ncols + j + 1,
347:                             (i + 1) * ncols + j,
348:                         ],
349:                         neighbors: [-1, -1, -1, -1],
350:                         normal: Some([th.cos(), 0.0, th.sin()]),
351:                     });
352:                 }
353:             }
354:             let fi: Vec<usize> = (0..faces.len()).collect();
355:             let vs: Vec<usize> = (0..vertices.len()).collect();
356:             let (origin, dir, radius) = fit_cylinder(&fi, &vs, &faces, &vertices)
357:                 .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("{nrows}x{ncols} arc should fit a cylinder"));
358:             assert!(
359:                 (radius - r).abs() < 1e-9,
360:                 "{nrows}x{ncols}: radius should be {r}, got {radius}"
361:             );
362:             assert!(
363:                 dir[1].abs() > 1.0 - 1e-9,
364:                 "{nrows}x{ncols}: axis should be along Y, got {dir:?}"
365:             );
366:             let worst = vs
367:                 .iter()
368:                 .map(|&v| vertex_to_cylinder_distance(&vertices[v], &origin, &dir, radius).abs())
369:                 .fold(0.0_f64, f64::max);
370:             assert!(worst < 1e-9, "{nrows}x{ncols}: worst vertex residual {worst:.3e}");
371:         }
372:     }
373: 
374:     /// Generate points on a cylinder: n_rings rings of n_pts_per_ring points.
375:     fn cylinder_points(
376:         axis_point: [f64; 3],
377:         axis_dir: [f64; 3],
378:         radius: f64,
379:         n_rings: usize,
380:         n_pts_per_ring: usize,
381:         h_min: f64,
382:         h_max: f64,
383:     ) -> Vec<MeshVertex> {
384:         let d = {
385:             let len = (axis_dir[0].powi(2) + axis_dir[1].powi(2) + axis_dir[2].powi(2)).sqrt();
386:             [axis_dir[0] / len, axis_dir[1] / len, axis_dir[2] / len]
387:         };
388:         let (u, w) = super::super::perpendicular_basis(&d);
389:         let mut pts = Vec::new();
390:         for ring in 0..n_rings {
391:             let t = if n_rings == 1 { 0.5 } else { ring as f64 / (n_rings - 1) as f64 };
392:             let h = h_min + t * (h_max - h_min);
393:             for j in 0..n_pts_per_ring {
394:                 let theta = 2.0 * PI * j as f64 / n_pts_per_ring as f64;
395:                 let (sin_t, cos_t) = theta.sin_cos();
396:                 pts.push(MeshVertex::from_xyz(
397:                     axis_point[0] + h * d[0] + radius * (cos_t * u[0] + sin_t * w[0]),
398:                     axis_point[1] + h * d[1] + radius * (cos_t * u[1] + sin_t * w[1]),
399:                     axis_point[2] + h * d[2] + radius * (cos_t * u[2] + sin_t * w[2]),
400:                 ));
401:             }
402:         }
403:         pts
404:     }
405: 
406:     /// Generate face normals for a cylinder (radially outward).
407:     fn cylinder_normals(axis_dir: [f64; 3], n_faces: usize) -> Vec<[f64; 3]> {
408:         let d = {
409:             let len = (axis_dir[0].powi(2) + axis_dir[1].powi(2) + axis_dir[2].powi(2)).sqrt();
410:             [axis_dir[0] / len, axis_dir[1] / len, axis_dir[2] / len]
411:         };
412:         let (u, w) = super::super::perpendicular_basis(&d);
413:         (0..n_faces)
414:             .map(|i| {
415:                 let theta = 2.0 * PI * i as f64 / n_faces as f64;
416:                 let (sin_t, cos_t) = theta.sin_cos();
417:                 [cos_t * u[0] + sin_t * w[0],
418:                  cos_t * u[1] + sin_t * w[1],
419:                  cos_t * u[2] + sin_t * w[2]]
420:             })
421:             .collect()
422:     }
423: 
424:     /// Build a synthetic cylinder mesh suitable for fit_cylinder.
425:     /// Returns (faces, vertices) where faces have normals and vertex_indices set.
426:     fn build_cylinder_mesh(
427:         axis_point: [f64; 3],
428:         axis_dir: [f64; 3],
429:         radius: f64,
430:         n_rings: usize,
431:         n_pts_per_ring: usize,
432:         h_min: f64,
433:         h_max: f64,
434:     ) -> (Vec<MeshFace>, Vec<MeshVertex>) {
435:         let vertices = cylinder_points(axis_point, axis_dir, radius, n_rings, n_pts_per_ring, h_min, h_max);
436:         let normals = cylinder_normals(axis_dir, n_rings * n_pts_per_ring);
437:         // Create one face per vertex (using adjacent vertices as a triangle)
438:         let mut faces = Vec::new();
439:         for ring in 0..n_rings.saturating_sub(1) {
440:             for j in 0..n_pts_per_ring {
441:                 let j_next = (j + 1) % n_pts_per_ring;
442:                 let i0 = ring * n_pts_per_ring + j;
443:                 let i1 = ring * n_pts_per_ring + j_next;
444:                 let i2 = (ring + 1) * n_pts_per_ring + j;
445:                 let fidx = faces.len();
446:                 faces.push(MeshFace {
447:                     vertex_count: 3,
448:                     vertex_indices: [i0, i1, i2, 0],
449:                     neighbors: [-1, -1, -1, -1],
450:                     normal: Some(normals[fidx % normals.len()]),
451:                 });
452:             }
453:         }
454:         (faces, vertices)
455:     }
456:

1: //! Stage 2.1: Planar hypothesis deduction and comparison.
2: 
3: use std::collections::{HashMap, HashSet};
4: 
5: 
6: use crate::stage1::{ConnectedMesh, MeshFace, MeshVertex};
7: 
8: use super::{
9:     face_area,
10:     PlanarHypothesis,
11: };
12: 
13: // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
14: // Planar fitting helpers
15: // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
16: 
17: /// Fit a plane to a set of faces: area-weighted normal averaging, and an
18: /// area-weighted vertex centroid for the distance.
19: ///
20: /// The area weighting on the offset matters as much as it does on the normal.
21: /// An unweighted vertex mean gives every vertex an equal vote, which is a
22: /// discretization artifact — it over-weights whatever part of the surface the
23: /// tessellator happened to sample densely. The continuous least-squares plane
24: /// minimizes ∫(n·x − d)² dA, and weighting each vertex by the area it stands
25: /// for is that integral's discretization.
26: ///
27: /// It also makes the fit robust to a small-area leak, which is what exposed
28: /// this: the end cap of `stepped_block_toroidal_fillets` is a handful of large
29: /// flat quads ringed by the many tiny quads of its perimeter fillet's first
30: /// row, 9.5e-4 mm off the plane. Those rows carry ~1% of the area but about
31: /// half the vertices, so the unweighted mean placed the plane at the *midpoint*
32: /// of the two levels — every one of its 95 faces 4.7e-4 mm out, with no
33: /// outlier group for anything downstream to spot.
34: pub(super) fn fit_plane(
35:     face_indices: &[usize],
36:     vertex_set: &HashSet<usize>,
37:     faces: &[MeshFace],
38:     vertices: &[MeshVertex],
39: ) -> ([f64; 3], f64) {
40:     let mut sum_nx = 0.0_f64;
41:     let mut sum_ny = 0.0_f64;
42:     let mut sum_nz = 0.0_f64;
43: 
44:     for &fi in face_indices {
45:         let face = &faces[fi];
46:         let n = face.normal.unwrap();
47:         let area = face_area(face, vertices);
48:         sum_nx += n[0] * area;
49:         sum_ny += n[1] * area;
50:         sum_nz += n[2] * area;
51:     }
52: 
53:     let len = (sum_nx * sum_nx + sum_ny * sum_ny + sum_nz * sum_nz).sqrt();
54:     let normal = [sum_nx / len, sum_ny / len, sum_nz / len];
55: 
56:     // Each vertex carries the area of its incident faces within the support,
57:     // shared equally among that face's corners.
58:     let mut weight: HashMap<usize, f64> = HashMap::with_capacity(vertex_set.len());
59:     for &fi in face_indices {
60:         let face = &faces[fi];
61:         let vc = face.vertex_count as usize;
62:         let share = face_area(face, vertices) / vc as f64;
63:         for &vi in &face.vertex_indices[..vc] {
64:             if vertex_set.contains(&vi) {
65:                 *weight.entry(vi).or_insert(0.0) += share;
66:             }
67:         }
68:     }
69:     let mut sum_d = 0.0_f64;
70:     let mut sum_w = 0.0_f64;
71:     for &vi in vertex_set {
72:         let v = &vertices[vi];
73:         let w = weight.get(&vi).copied().unwrap_or(0.0);
74:         sum_d += w * (normal[0] * v.x + normal[1] * v.y + normal[2] * v.z);
75:         sum_w += w;
76:     }
77:     let distance = if sum_w > 0.0 {
78:         sum_d / sum_w
79:     } else {
80:         // Degenerate support (zero total area) — fall back to the plain mean.
81:         vertex_set
82:             .iter()
83:             .map(|&vi| {
84:                 let v = &vertices[vi];
85:                 normal[0] * v.x + normal[1] * v.y + normal[2] * v.z
86:             })
87:             .sum::<f64>()
88:             / vertex_set.len().max(1) as f64
89:     };
90: 
91:     (normal, distance)
92: }
93: 
94: // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
95: // Stage 2.1: Compare check
96: // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
97: 
98: // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
99: // SurfaceType trait implementation for generic BFS
100: // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
101: 
102: use super::surface::SurfaceType;
103: 
104: /// Fitted plane parameters.
105: #[derive(Clone)]
106: pub(super) struct PlaneFit {
107:     pub normal: [f64; 3],
108:     pub distance: f64,
109: }
110: 
111: /// Marker struct for the planar surface type.
112: pub(super) struct PlanarSurface;
113: 
114: impl SurfaceType for PlanarSurface {
115:     type Fit = PlaneFit;
116:     type Hypothesis = PlanarHypothesis;
117: 
118:     fn fit_surface(
119:         mesh: &ConnectedMesh,
120:         face_list: &[usize],
121:         vertex_list: &[usize],
122:         _verbosity: u8,
123:     ) -> Option<Self::Fit> {
124:         let vertex_set: HashSet<usize> = vertex_list.iter().copied().collect();
125:         let (normal, distance) = fit_plane(face_list, &vertex_set, &mesh.faces, &mesh.vertices);
126:         // The area-weighted normal of a closed or symmetric region (a full
127:         // cylindrical hole wall) cancels to ~zero and normalizes to NaN. A
128:         // non-finite plane would then VACUOUSLY validate — every `dist > vtol`
129:         // test is false for NaN — and be committed over the cylinder the
130:         // region actually is. Reject it so the ladder falls through.
131:         if !normal.iter().all(|c| c.is_finite()) || !distance.is_finite() {
132:             return None;
133:         }
134:         Some(PlaneFit { normal, distance })
135:     }
136: 
137:     fn vertex_distance(fit: &Self::Fit, pos: &[f64; 3]) -> f64 {
138:         (fit.normal[0] * pos[0] + fit.normal[1] * pos[1] + fit.normal[2] * pos[2] - fit.distance)
139:             .abs()
140:     }
141: 
142:     fn make_hypothesis(
143:         mesh: &ConnectedMesh,
144:         faces: Vec<usize>,
145:         vertices: Vec<usize>,
146:         fit: &Self::Fit,
147:     ) -> PlanarHypothesis {
148:         let mut error_max = f64::NEG_INFINITY;
149:         let mut error_min = f64::INFINITY;
150:         let mut error_abs_sum = 0.0_f64;
151:         for &vi in &vertices {
152:             let v = &mesh.vertices[vi];
153:             let d = fit.normal[0] * v.x + fit.normal[1] * v.y + fit.normal[2] * v.z
154:                 - fit.distance;
155:             error_max = error_max.max(d);
156:             error_min = error_min.min(d);
157:             error_abs_sum += d.abs();
158:         }
159:         PlanarHypothesis {
160:             normal: fit.normal,
161:             distance: fit.distance,
162:             faces,
163:             vertices,
164:             error_max,
165:             error_min,
166:             error_abs_sum,
167:         }
168:     }
169: }
